- **dialogue-history**: Maintain the change-history plane of a Dialogue Knowledge Repository: reconcile divergent Change Records with a Merge, and Pack the closed history of a long-lived repository into fewer files without losing the canonical ledger. Use when concurrent branches advanced the same target and their revisions diverged, when a repository accumulates too many Change Record files, or when initializing a repository's history.pack policy. Complements dialogue-knowledge, which manages the current-state plane. ([epitedeuma/dialogue](https://github.com/epitedeuma/dialogue/tree/HEAD/skills/dialogue-history))
- **dialogue-infer-from-code**: Infer requirements, decisions, and assumptions from code artifacts. Use after code generation or when the user has built something without prior elicitation. Enables "Build First" mode by reverse-capturing graph content from implementation. Triggers on "what did we just build", "capture from code", "infer requirements", "what assumptions did we make", "document what we built". ([plucins/tool-eval](https://github.com/plucins/tool-eval/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin-evo/skills/dialogue-infer-from-code))

- **dialogue-knowledge**: Discover a project's shared Knowledge Repository from .dialogue.yml, resolve current project knowledge, and safely create or edit managed documents with semantic change history. Use when an agent needs shared plans, designs, decisions, tasks, or progress, and automatically when a conversation establishes or approves a durable project decision, requirement, design, constraint, priority, or lifecycle change, even if the user does not name this Skill. ([epitedeuma/dialogue](https://github.com/epitedeuma/dialogue/tree/HEAD/skills/dialogue-knowledge))

- **dialogue-polish**: OPRN 공식 윤문 스킬. ([ur-grue/autopunk-media-skills](https://github.com/ur-grue/autopunk-media-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/screenwriting/scripting/dialogue-polisher) · ★46)
- **dialogue-principles**: 依頼者と議論・対話を進める場面で必ず発火させる skill。共創を目指して、認識を小さく揃えながら、同じ抽象度・レイヤーで話すための対話の進め方を扱う。要件・設計フェーズの対話、実装中の設計判断の議論、レビュー結果の共有、複数の論点・選択肢を依頼者に渡す場面、依頼者からの指摘・反論に応答する場面、「確認したい」「議論したい」「相談したい」と問いかけたいとき、いずれも発火対象。「会話」ではなく「対話」を成立させたい全場面で効く。質問がスルーされる・訂正が続く・話題を変えられる・「そうじゃなくて」と返されるなど、噛み合っていない兆候を観測したときは軌道修正のために再度発火させる。 ([sei-newbear/xp-harness](https://github.com/sei-newbear/xp-harness/tree/HEAD/.apm/skills/dialogue-principles) · ★9)
- **dialogue-progressive-capture**: Describes how the dialogue-agent progressively populates the context graph with new users and projects. - **dialogue-refine**: 将 AI 对话记录提炼、脱敏并重构为结构化的 Hexo 博客加工稿。用于从对话生成博客文章、隐藏业务敏感细节、依据用户本机 blogRoot 定位素材，并将临时加工稿安全交给 hexo-push 发布。 ([askairo/kairo-skills](https://github.com/askairo/kairo-skills/tree/HEAD/content/blog/dialogue-refine) · ★1)
